To compute gradient for all i, for each shift l, weight w_l, contribution to indices i,l: to i (as n) -w_l h_{i+l}; to i+l (as j) +w_l(1-h_{i+l}). This is sparse O(N^2) if loop. But can compute via FFT: The vector A_i = Σ_l w_{i-l}(1-h_{i-l})? hmm.
Let Δc for ε at i: Δc_l = (u_{i-l}) - v_{i+l}, where u_m = 1-h_m if i-l=m (l=i-m), v_m = h_m if i+l=m (l=m-i). Thus as a function of shift l, Δc = permuted (1-h_{i-l}) for negative l and -h_{i+l} for positive l.

Let's implement a robust iterative projection + active subgradient method.

Detailed design of projected gradient:

Global variables:
_START_TIME, _BUDGET_S, _BEST_H, _BEST_C, _BEST_N? Could use local in run but helpers need access? We can pass.

Helper functions top-level:
- compute_c5(h): exact with np.correlate and sum check? Use dx=2/len(h), c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); return float(np.max(c)*dx).
- correlate_full_fft(h): returns c full length 2n-1 using FFT. We'll need for objective and gradient.
- c5_from_corr(c, n): return max(c)*2/n.
- softmax_weights(c, beta, dx): C = c*dx; shift max; exp(beta*(C-Cmax)); normalize. Return weights (sum 1), maybe phi = (Cmax+log(sum exp)/beta)*? Actually phi = (Cmax + log(sum exp)/beta) because divide by exp max? φ= (Cmax + (1/β)log Σ exp(β(C-Cmax))). Use for objective.
- smooth_obj_and_grad(h, beta, exact_flag=False): compute c (fft), weights, phi, grad. But to avoid recomputing in line search, separate.
- grad_from_weights(h, wfull, dx): compute gradient of φ (unnormalized weights normalized). Uses loop.
- project_to_box_sum(y, target): bisection.
- project_bounds_y(y): just clip.
- make_anchor? 

Line search for projected gradient:
```
def projected_step(h, grad, current_C, current_phi, beta, max_evals=...):
  n=len(h); target=n/2
  gnorm = np.linalg.norm(grad)
  if gnorm ==0: return h,False
  base = 1.0/(gnorm + 1e-12) maybe times factor 0.5
  for alpha in alphas:
     y = h - alpha*grad
     x = project_box_sum(y,target)
     c = corrf(x); C=c5; phi=...
     if C < current_C - eps or phi < current_phi - 1e-12*...:
         return x, True
```
But computing phi requires weights; can compute c5 only and use descent in φ approx. We can compute φ from c quickly and weights? Too costly. Use Armijo: accept if phi_new < phi_cur - c1*alpha*||grad||^2. Need grad norm. But we approximate. We can also accept if C improves. To avoid recomputing weights, compute c and phi via formula from c (no weights? φ = Cmax + logsumexp/beta). This is cheap O(N). Good.
So line search compute c, C, φ.
Accept if C < current_C - 1e-15 or φ < current_phi - 1e-12 - 1e-10? But exact C may not monotonically follow φ. We can set h_new = x if φ decreased (descent) OR C improved; if φ not decreased but C improved, still ok. If neither, try smaller.
If no alpha works, maybe take very small step? Or skip.

FFT correlate implementation:
```
def correlate_full_fft(a, v):
    n=len(a)
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B=np.fft.rfft(v, L)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(B), L)
    c=np.empty(2*n-1)
    if n>1:
       c[:n-1] = r[L-(n-1):L][::-1]
    c[n-1] = r[0]
    if n>1: c[n:]=r[1:n]
    return c
```

Gradient loop function:
```
def grad_smooth(h, wfull, dx):
    n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n); g += wfull[n-1]*(1-2*h)
    for p in range(1,n):
        wp = wfull[n-1+p]; wm = wfull[n-1-p]
        if wp !=0 or wm !=0:
           hb = h[:n-p]; hs = h[p:]
           g[:n-p] += wm*(1-hs) - wp*hs
           g[p:n] += wp*(1-hb) - wm*hb
    return g
```
Is this gradient for φ with weights normalized over c? Derivative φ = Σ w_l ∂c_l * dx if w from softmax C? Let's set wfull = exp(β C)/sum; φ=(1/β)log sum exp βC. dφ/dh = (1/sum) Σ exp(βC) dC = Σ wfull dC. dC=dx dc. So multiply by dx. The loop above gives dφ/dh? It sums w*dc; need *dx. So return dx * (...). We'll include dx outside or inside. In line search, grad must be correct scale. Use `grad = dx * grad_raw`.
For p=0, ∂c0/∂h=1-2h. Good.
Test with simple 2 variables? h=[a,b], n=2. c full:
lag -1: h0(1-h1)
0: a(1-a)+b(1-b)
+1: h1(1-h0)
Weights wm, w0, wp. Loop p=1:
hb=h0,hs=h1
g0 += wm*(1-h1) - wp*h1
g1 += wp*(1-h0) - wm*h0
plus w0(1-2h). Let's check derivative wrt a:
c_- = a(1-b) derivative 1-b -> wm*(1-b)
c0 derivative 1-2a
c_+ = b(1-a) derivative -b -> -wp*b. matches. Good.

Smooth objective:
```
def smooth_phi(c, beta, dx):
   C = c*dx
   m = C.max()
   if beta <=0: return m
   e = np.exp(beta*(C-m))
   return m + np.log(e.sum())/beta
```
Weights: `z=np.exp(beta*(C-m)); w=z/z.sum()`.

def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    # if y sum close target and within bounds return clip
    lo = np.min(y) - 1.0
    hi = np.max(y) + 1.0? Need sum decreasing in lam: x=clip(y-lam,0,1). At lam -> -inf sum=N, lam -> inf sum=0. We want target in [0,N]. Set lo=np.min(y)-1? If lam very negative, x clipped 1 for y-lam. Easier use bisection on lam in [min(y)-1, max(y)+1]? At lam=min(y)-1, y-lam>=1 for min? All y-lam >=? if y≥min, y-(min-1)=1+(y-min)≥1 => sum=N.     for 60: mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0,1)); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid.
    x=clip(y-(lo+hi)/2)
    # adjust tiny diff by adding to non-bound
    diff=target-x.sum(); if diff>0: mask=x<1; x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum(); x=np.clip; else ...
    return x
```

Implement `pair_exchange_polish(h, max_sweeps)`:
```
for sweep:
  c=corr_fft; phi? grad? We can compute raw gradient using active top weights (softmax beta=1000 or uniform top). Then order variables by grad.
  For a in first Q largest grad, b in last Q smallest grad:

`compute_c5_exact(h)`:
```
def compute_c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
Need ensure h feasible clipped? Could call within.

`correlate_full(h)`:
 We can choose FFT for speed; but final exact uses np.correlate. To avoid overflow/round, use double. For N up to 2048. Implement with rfft.
```
def correlate_full_fft(h):
    n=len(h)
    v=1.0-h
    L=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
    B=np.fft.rfft(v,L)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(B),L)
    r = np.where(r<0 & >-? maybe) # no clip? Could tiny negative.
    c=np.empty(2*n-1)
    if n>1:
       c[:n-1]=r[L-(n-1):L]
       c[n-1]=r[0]
       c[n:]=r[1:n]
    else: c[0]=r[0]
    # maybe set tiny negative to 0? c_nonneg; clip at 0 for objective to avoid round exp issues
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0
    c[(c<0)&(c>-1e-12)]=0
    return c
```
Clipping negative small okay; if numerical negative large? no.

`c5_from_corr(c,n)`: return max*2/n.

`smooth_values(c,n,beta)`: C=c*2/n; max; e=np.exp beta*(C-max); sum; phi=max+log(sum)/beta; w=e/sum.

Line search function:
```
def line_search_phi(h, grad, c_cur, phi_cur, beta, best_c_global=None):
    n=len(h); target=n/2
    gnorm2 = np.dot(grad,grad)
    if gnorm2 <=1e-30: return h, c_cur, phi_cur, False
    alpha = 1.0
    c1=1e-4
    for _ in range(12):
        y=h-alpha*grad
        x=project_box_sum(y,target)
        c=correlate_full_fft(x)
        C=c5_from_corr(c,n)
        phi=smooth_phi(c,n,beta)
        if C < c_cur - 1e-12 or phi < phi_cur - c1*alpha*gnorm2:
            return x,C,phi,True
        alpha*=0.5
    return h,c_cur,phi_cur,False
```
But if beta large and phi has scale, c1*alpha*gnorm2 may be tiny; accept. If no descent due projection, we might still accept if C_new < best_global? But h may not descent phi; maybe still improve. Pair exchange polish:
```
def pair_exchange_polish(h, time_limit, rng):
  n=len(h); target=n/2
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5_exact(best_h)? We'll use local and record best.
  for sweep in range(1000):
    if time<5 break
    c=correlate_full_fft(best_h); C=...
    # compute active top gradient weights with high beta or top active for selecting order
    wfull=np.zeros; active top? use softmax beta=1000 for ordering.
    grad=grad_smooth(best_h,wfull,n) # dx scaled
    idx_grad = np.argsort(grad) # low grad first (increase), high last
    cand_low = idx_grad[:Q]; cand_high=idx_grad[-Q:]
    evaluated=0
    improved_sweep=False
    for i in cand_high:
      vi=best_h[i]
      for j in cand_low:
        if i==j: continue
        vj=best_h[j]
        max_delta = min(vi, 1.0-vj) # amount move: decrease i, increase j
        if max_delta <=1e-10: continue
        # try fractions
        for frac in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0):
           d=frac*max_delta
           x=best_h.copy(); x[i]-=d; x[j]+=d
           c=correlate_full_fft(x); Cx=c5
           if Cx < best_c - 1e-12:
               best_h=x; best_c=Cx; record; improved_sweep=True; break
        # maybe opposite direction if grad? but grad told direction.
    if not improved_sweep: break
  return best_h,best_c
```

def slsqp_polish(h, beta, maxiter=200):
  global _GLOBAL_OPT_N, _GLOBAL_OPT_BETA

def improve_full(h, time_limit, beta_schedule, pair=True, slsqq=False):
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0

    # exact initial record
    c_exact=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,c_exact)
    for beta in beta_schedule:
        if remaining() < max(10, 5*beta_schedule.index? ) break
        if beta small and not enough total? Use at most.
        c,phi = ...
        improved_iters=0
        for it in range(5000):
            if remaining() < 2: break
            _,w=softmax_weights(c,n,beta)
            grad=grad_from_weights(h,w,n)
            gnorm2=np.dot(grad,grad)
            if gnorm2 < 1e-26: break
            h_new,c_new,phi_new,ok = line_search_full(h,grad,c,c5_from_corr(c,n),phi,beta,time_limit)
            if not ok:

                h_pert = project_box_sum(h + 0.01*(rng? no rng passed), target)
                if compute_c5_exact? maybe break beta
                break
            h,c,phi = h_new,c_new,phi_new
            if it % 5 == 0:
                if n <= 512:
                   cx=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,cx)
                else:
                   # fft c gives approx; record if significantly lower approximate? But exact needed for bound; maybe compute exact every 20.
                   cx=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,cx)
        # end iters
    # pair polish

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
 global _START_TIME,_BUDGET_S,_REC_H,_REC_C,_REC_N

 # generate anti sine patterns at 160/320

   for amp,phase in [(0.5,0),(0.5,np.pi/4),(0.45,np.pi/3),(0.4,0)]: cands.append make_anti_sine(n,amp,phase)

 # improve each with alloc based on n and position

Pseudo:
```
def line_search_full(h, grad, c, C, phi, beta, target):
    gnorm2=np.dot(grad,grad)
    if gnorm2<=1e-30: return h,c,C,phi,False
    alpha=1.0
    best_x=None; best_phi=phi; best_c=C
    for _ in range(14):
       x=project_box_sum(h-alpha*grad, target)
       c_new=correlate_full_fft(x)
       C_new=c5_from_corr(c_new,len(h))
       phi_new=smooth_phi(c_new,len(h),beta)
       if phi_new < best_phi - 1e-15:
           best_phi=phi_new; best_c=C_new; best_x=x; best_c_new=c_new
       if C_new < C - 1e-13 or phi_new < phi - 1e-4*alpha*gnorm2:
           return x,c_new,C_new,phi_new,True
       alpha*=0.5
    if best_x is not None and best_phi < phi - 1e-14:
       return best_x, best_c_new, best_c, best_phi, True
    return h,c,C,phi,False
```
Armijo constant maybe 1e-4 too strict for flat; use 1e-6. Also phi scale ~0.3, gnorm2 maybe 1e-4, alpha*gnorm2 ~1e-4, 1e-4* =>1e-8. Good.
If phi decreases but not Armijo, accept after loop if best. Good.

`project_box_sum` bisection:
```
def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.array(y, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if y.size == 0: return y
    # if all within [-1e-8,1+1e-8], quick
    if y.min() >= -1e-9 and y.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-9:
        x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
        hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
            s = np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - np.sum(x)
    # correction loop: distribute to non-saturated
    for _ in range(8):
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = x < 1.0 - 1e-12
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt == 0: break
        x[mask] += diff/cnt
        x = np.clip(x,0,1)
        diff = target - np.sum(x)
    return x
```
If n odd and target .5, exact sum possible with fractional. Correction loop may not hit exact if many at bounds; but diff small. Could adjust one variable more precisely:
```
if abs(diff)>0:
   if diff>0:
      # add to x with least increase to bound
      i=int(np.argmin(1-x)); x[i]+=diff; if >1 adjust...
```
But likely not needed. For feasibility exact, `sum(h)==n/2 exactly` in floating? They likely check tolerance. We can set one value to exact difference:
After correction, if abs(diff)>1e-13:
 choose index with room sign; x[i] = min(max(x[i]+diff,0),1); then recompute? If appended exceeds, distribute. Simpler not.

Since target N/2 integer for even, sum of floats maybe 80.00000000000001. Fine.

FFT correlate `L=1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()`; if n=0? no.

`smooth_phi`: If c contains tiny negative? c nonnegative. `C=c*(2.0/n)`. `maxC = float(np.max(C))`. For beta maybe large, `beta*(C-maxC)` >= -beta*(range) maybe -10*1? For beta 3000, range < maybe 0.2 -> -6, underflow okay; use `np.exp` might underflow many, fine. If all underflow? max term 1.

`softmax_weights`: Similar. Return `z/s`, phi maybe not. We can combine to avoid recompute:
```
def softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta):
  C=c*(2/n); m=C.max(); z=np.exp(beta*(C-m)); s=z.sum(); phi=m+math.log(s)/beta; return phi, z/s
```
If beta==0: phi=m, w uniform? Use uniform over all? But not.

`grad_from_weights`: Use normalized weights; include dx. For speed, if n small, loop all.
```
def grad_from_weights(h,wfull,n):
  dx=2.0/n
  g=np.zeros(n)
  w0=wfull[n-1]
  if w0: g += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
  base_idx=n-1
  for p in range(1,n):
     wp=wfull[base_idx+p]; wm=wfull[base_idx-p]
     if wp <=1e-16 and wm<=1e-16: continue
     hb=h[:n-p]; hs=h[p:]
     g[:n-p] += wm*(1.0-hs) - wp*hs
     g[p:n] += wp*(1.0-hb) - wm*hb
  return g*dx
```
This computes raw weighted derivative with weights normalized. Good.

Potential memory: `1.0-hs` creates arrays each p. Could optimize by precomputing `one_minus_h = 1-h`. Then `om_hb=1-h[:,?]?`. For p, need 1-h_sb and 1-h_base. Use `omh=1.0-h`.
```
omh=1-h
for p:
  hb=h[:n-p]; hs=h[p:]; omhb=omh[:n-p]; omhs=omh[p:]
  g[:n-p] += wm*omhs - wp*hs
  g[p:n] += wp*omhb - wm*hb
```
Avoid recompute. Good.

`make_anti_h(z)`:
```
def make_anti_h(z):
  m=len(z); h=np.empty(2*m); h[:m]=z; h[m:]=1-z; return h
```

Testing mentally for orientation and gradient. If gradient sign wrong, projected gradient will increase max and local exact might not improve; but pair polish uses gradient selection: we assume variables with high grad should be decreased, low increased. If sign reversed, pair polish would hurt but exact acceptance prevents updates (won't accept worse). Projected gradient could harm but local best keeps. SLSQP with wrong jac would fail maybe. Need ensure sign. Let's test with simple h=[0,1] N=2. dx=1. h sum=1? Wait target n/2=1, okay. c full:
lag -1: h0(1-h1)=0
0: h0(1-h0)+h1(1-h1)=0
+1: h1(1-h0)=1
C all [0,0,1], max=1.
Weights: wpos[1]=1, others0. Gradient formula: p=1, wp=1, wm=0, hb=h0=0, hs=h1=1.
g0 += wm*omhs - wp*hs = -1
g1 += wp*omhb - wm*hb = 1*(1-0)=1
grad dx same [-1,1]. If we do h - grad = [1,0], objective c lag? h=[1,0]: c_+ = h1(1-h0)=0, c_- = h0(1-h1)=1, max1. No improvement because max same. If take half disk? h=[0.5,0.5], c0=0.5, max0.5 actually improves. Step h - α grad with α=0.5 -> [0.5,0.5], good. Sign correct: high h1 decreased, low h0 increased. Good.

Another test h=[1,0], c_-? h=[1,0]: lag -1: h0(1-h1)=1, +1=0. Weights wm=1. Gradient: p=1 wm=1, wp=0: g0 +=1*omhs (omhs=1) =1; g1 += -wm*hb = -1. Step h - grad -> [0,1] no max change; small step -> [1-α, α] c? balanced. Good.

Line search alpha=1 from [0,1] gives [1,0] same C; phi for beta=100 maybe same? Both max1, but c distribution? [0,1] c [0,0,1], [1,0] c [1,0,0]; softmax max same. No descent. α=0.5 gives balanced C max0.5, descent. Good.

Projection with sum=1: from [0,1] - step grad [ -1,1]*0.5 -> [0.5,0.5]. Good.

So gradient likely correct.

c = np.real(c)

`softmax_and_phi`:
```
def softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta):
    dx=2.0/n
    C=c*dx
    if beta <= 0:
       m=float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0
       w=np.full(C.size,1.0/C.size)
       return m,w
    m=float(np.max(C))
    z=np.exp(beta*(C-m))
    s=float(np.sum(z))
    if s <=0: z=np.zeros; z[np.argmax(C)]=1; s=1
    phi=m + math.log(s)/beta
    return phi, z/s
```
If beta large, `math.log(s)` where s≥1 due max term exp0=1; okay. If C includes NaN? If h not finite, handle.

`grad_from_weights`: if wfull sum not 1? It is. For n=2, base_idx=1, p=1 index2 ok.

`project_box_sum`: Potential if target outside [0,n] due? target n/2. Fine.

`resample_h`: After interpolation, sum may not target; project. If src empty? no.

`make_anti_from_h`: if n<2 return? For odd, m=n//2; z=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m:2*m]); h2 length2*m. If initial parent odd, use first 2*m. If parent length maybe 160.

`make_anti_sine(n, amp, phase)`: if n odd? ensure even: `if n % 2: n += 1`. m=n//2.
Use centers: x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m.
`z=0.5 + amp*np.sin(np.pi*x + phase)`. If amp >0.5, clip.

`make_tent(n)`: if n odd? even? project. `x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n`; `h=np.minimum(x,2-x)`. Clip, project.
Note tent sum exactly? continuous; project.

`make_thue_morse(n)`: Use parity efficient? Simpler loop. Density near half; project. Could be binary but sum integer if n even? Thue-Morse length even may have exactly n/2? For n power of 2 yes; for arbitrary maybe not. project.

`line_search_full` requires `target=n/2`. Return tuple. Implementation:
```
def line_search_full(h, grad, c, C, phi, beta, target):
    n=len(h)
    gnorm2=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
    if gnorm2 <= 1e-30: return h,c,C,phi,False
    alpha=1.0
    best_x=None; best_carray=None; best_C=C; best_phi=phi
    # cap alpha? if gnorm small, alpha huge? gnorm2 maybe 1e-12, alpha=1. If grad norm=1e-6, alpha 1 step 1e-6; ok. If grad max=0.1, alpha=1 step 0.1.
    for _ in range(14):
       x=project_box_sum(h - alpha*grad, target)
       cx=correlate_full_fft(x)
       Cx=c5_from_corr(cx,n)
       phix=smooth_phi(cx,n,beta)
       if phix < best_phi - 1e-15:
          best_x=x; best_carray=cx; best_C=Cx; best_phi=phix
       if Cx < C - 1e-13 or phix < phi - 1e-6*alpha*gnorm2:
          return x,cx,Cx,phix,True
       alpha *= 0.5
    if best_x is not None and best_phi < phi - 1e-14:
       return best_x,best_carray,best_C,best_phi,True
    return h,c,C,phi,False
```
Armijo constant 1e-6. If alpha gets tiny, break. Good.

Could use `q = alpha*gnorm2` but if grad units and phi, Armijo. Fine.

`line_search_anti` similar with `project_box`.

`improve_full` beta schedule. `eval_every` choose based on n: `eval_every = 5 if n <= 256 else (10 if n <=512 else 20)`.
Loop:
```
def improve_full(h, alloc, beta_sched, rng, do_pair=True):
    deadline=time.time()+alloc
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=correlate_full_fft(h); C=c5_from_corr(c,n); Cex=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,Cex)
    local= h.copy(); local_c=Cex
    for beta in beta_sched:
       if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 5: break
       phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)
       for it in range(10000):
          if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 3: break
          grad=grad_from_weights(h,w,n)
          h_new,c_new,C_new,phi_new,ok=line_search_full(h,grad,c,C,phi,beta,target)
          if not ok: break
          h,c,C,phi=h_new,c_new,C_new,phi_new
          w = None? Next loop will recompute. But we need w for next iteration; we can set `phi,w = softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)` after move? That costs. We can compute w after move to avoid stale. Do that.
          if C > local_c + 0.005:
              h=local.copy(); c=correlate_full_fft(h); C=local_c; phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)
              continue
          if it % eval_every == 4:
              Cex=compute_c5_exact(h)
              if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
                  local= h.copy(); local_c=Cex; record_best(h,Cex)
              # if current C much worse than local and no local update, reset? optional
          # update weights for next iter
          phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)
       # end iterations
       # exact at beta end
       Cex=compute_c5_exact(h)
       if Cex < local_c -1e-15: local=..., record
       if Cex > local_c + 0.002: h=local.copy()
    if do_pair and n <= 512 and time.time() < deadline-5 and remaining()>5:
       pair_time = min(60.0, deadline-time.time(), remaining()*0.4?)
       h2=local.copy()
       h2,c2=pair_exchange_polish(h2, max(pair_time, 2), rng)
       if c2 < local_c: local=h2; local_c=c2
    return local, local_c
```
Need avoid recomputing `phi,w` after every move too expensive? Each recompute O(N) exp; ok. But line search already computed c; we can use c. We need weights for next gradient. So recompute after accepted move. In for loop, we compute grad with old w; after move, recompute.
If no move, break.

`pair_exchange_polish`: Use h exact feasible. It can operate on any h, not anti. It updates record_best.
Implementation more concrete:
```
def pair_exchange_polish(h, time_limit, rng):
    deadline=time.time()+time_limit
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,c); local=h.copy(); local_c=c
    no_imp=0
    Q=8 if n<=200 else 6
    for sweep in range(10000):
       if time.time()>deadline or remaining()<5: break
       c_arr=correlate_full_fft(h); C=c5_from_corr(c_arr,n)
       phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c_arr,n,1000.0)
       grad=grad_from_weights(h,w,n)
       order=np.argsort(grad)
       lows=order[:Q]
       highs=order[-Q:]
       improved=False
       # To get diversity, if no improvement maybe add random swap? We'll after no_imp add random.
       for a in highs:
          ha=h[a]
          for b in lows:
             if a==b: continue
             hb=h[b]
             maxd=min(ha, 1.0-hb)
             if maxd <=1e-10: continue
             for frac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
                d=frac*maxd
                x=h.copy()
                x[a]-=d; x[b]+=d
                # exact sum preserved but due float maybe; no project
                cx=correlate_full_fft(x); Cx=c5_from_corr(cx,n)
                if Cx < local_c - 1e-10:
                   Cxex=compute_c5_exact(x)
                   if Cxex < local_c - 1e-12:
                      h=x; local_c=Cxex; local=h.copy(); record_best(h,local_c); improved=True
                      break
             if improved:
                 # maybe stop current pair direction? continue to next? we can continue to find more
                 pass
       if improved:
          no_imp=0
          # exact h already local
       else:
          no_imp += 1
          # sometimes random perturb two vars to escape
          if no_imp >= 2 and remaining()>10:
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
             if i!=j:
                x=h.copy(); x[i]=rng.rand(); x[j]=rng.rand(); x=project_box_sum(x,target)
                Cxex=compute_c5_exact(x)
                if Cxex < local_c -1e-12:
                   h=x; local=h.copy(); local_c=Cxex; record; no_imp=0
          elif no_imp >=3:
             break
    return local, local_c
```
This may be slow if many trials; we can limit trials per sweep by if time. It breaks fractions only after accept. For no improvement, 64*5=320 FFT per sweep. If 100 sweeps ->32k FFT. Fine.
Random perturbation rare.

Potential negative: local_c updated only exact; FFT prefilter threshold 1e-10. If exact improvement less than FFT threshold? FFT and exact close; okay.

Maybe use FFT `Cx < local_c - 1e-7` to trigger exact; then exact update threshold -1e-12. Use -1e-9.

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, beta, maxiter=100):
    global _SL_N,_SL_BETA
    n=len(h); target=n/2
    _SL_N=n; _SL_BETA=beta
    x0=project_box_sum(h,target)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slsqp_con}]
    try:
       res=minimize(_slsqp_obj, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'iprint':0})
       x=project_box_sum(res.x,target)
       return x
    except Exception:
       return x0
```
SLSQP may be slow; call only final. `_slsqp_obj` and jac use FFT. If `res.x` nan, return.

 # ensure sum? if not, maybe use record h if better? We only want feasible. Use h_out.

Potential issue with FFT correlate and `np.correlate` exact: The order we derived. Let's double-check with n=3 example: r positive? Our c = [r[L-n+1:L], r[0], r[1:n-1]]? Code: `c[:n-1] = r[L-(n-1):L]`; for n=3 L=4, c[0:2]=r[2:4]=[0,1]; c[2]=r0=2; c[3:]=r1:3? r[1:3]=[3,1]? Wait r[2]=0, r[3]=1, r[0]=2, r[1]=3. r[1:n] = r[1:3]=[3,1]. But c length 5: c[3:5]=[3,1]. Full expected [0,1,2,3,0]? Last should 0, not 1! Ah! I made mistake: r[2] negative lag -1? Let's recalc r for a=[1,2,3], v=[0,1,0].
L=4. a_padded [1,2,3,0], v [0,1,0,0]. r[k]=Σ a[i+k]v[i].

```
def grad_from_weights(h,wfull,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    if n >= 256:
       L=1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
       w=np.zeros(L)
       w_rev=np.zeros(L)
       w0=wfull[n-1]
       w[0]=w0; w_rev[0]=w0
       if n>1:
          w[1:n]=wfull[n:]
          w[L-(n-1):L]=wfull[:n-1]
          w_rev[1:n]=wfull[:n-1][::-1]
          w_rev[L-(n-1):L]=wfull[n:][::-1]
       q=1.0-h
       A=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w,L)*np.fft.rfft(q,L),L)[:n]
       B=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w_rev,L)*np.fft.rfft(h,L),L)[:n]
       g=dx*(A-B)
       # clip small? 
       return g
    else:
       ... loop
```

`record_best`:
```
def record_best(h, c):
    global _REC_H, _REC_C, _REC_N
    if h is None or len(h)==0: return
    try:
        hq = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h,dtype=float), float(len(h))/2.0)
        cq = float(c) if c is not None else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(cq) or cq > _REC_C:
            cq = compute_c5_exact(hq)
        if cq < _REC_C:
            _REC_H = hq.copy()
            _REC_C = cq
            _REC_N = len(hq)
    except Exception:
        pass
```
If c passed exact and lower but projection changes C higher, we compute c2? The logic above: if c not finite or c > _REC_C, compute c2 else use c. But if c < _REC_C yet projection changes to higher than _REC_C? Unlikely but possible if c from unprojected. To be safe, always compute `cq2=compute_c5_exact(hq)` and take min? But if c is FFT approximate lower but exact c2 higher, we should not record. We can just always recompute exact on projected h:
```
cq=compute_c5_exact(hq)
if cq < _REC_C: update
```

`compute_c5_exact`:
```
def compute_c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return inf
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return 1e100
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```

`correlate_full_fft` if h nonfinite return zeros? If nonfinite, objective inf. Add:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return np.full(2*n-1, np.inf)
```

`improve_full`: Need beta_sched not empty. If beta_sched list.
```
def improve_full(h, alloc, beta_sched, rng, do_pair=True):
    global _REC_H...
    deadline = time.time() + alloc
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=correlate_full_fft(h)
    C=c5_from_corr(c,n)
    record_best(h,C)
    local=h.copy(); local_c=compute_c5_exact(h)
    eval_every=5 if n<=256 else (10 if n<=512 else 20)
    for beta in beta_sched:
       if time.time()>deadline or remaining()<5: break
       if not math.isfinite(beta) or beta <=0: continue
       phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)
       if not math.isfinite(phi): break
       for it in range(10000):
          if time.time()>deadline or remaining()<3: break
          grad=grad_from_weights(h,w,n)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
          h_new,c_new,C_new,phi_new,ok=line_search_full(h,grad,c,C,phi,beta,target)
          if not ok: break
          h,c,C,phi=h_new,c_new,C_new,phi_new
          if C > local_c + 0.005:
             h=local.copy(); c=correlate_full_fft(h); C=local_c
          phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c,n,beta)
          if not math.isfinite(phi): break
          if it % eval_every == eval_every-1:
             Cex=compute_c5_exact(h)
             if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
                local=h.copy(); local_c=Cex; record_best(h,Cex)
             elif Cex > local_c + 0.003:
                h=local.copy(); c=correlate_full_fft(h); C=local_c
       Cex=compute_c5_exact(h)
       if Cex < local_c -1e-15: local=h.copy(); local_c=Cex; record_best(h,Cex)
       elif Cex > local_c +0.002: h=local.copy()
    if do_pair and n <= 512 and time.time() < deadline - 5 and remaining()>5:
       pair_time = min(60.0, max(5.0, deadline-time.time()))
       h2=local.copy()
       h2,c2=pair_exchange_polish(h2, pair_time, rng)
       if c2 < local_c: local=h2; local_c=c2
    return local, local_c
```

```
def pair_exchange_polish(h, time_limit, rng):
    deadline=time.time()+time_limit
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    local=h.copy(); local_c=compute_c5_exact(h); record_best(h,local_c)
    no_imp=0
    Q=8 if n<=200 else 6
    for sweep in range(10000):
       if time.time()>deadline or remaining()<5: break
       c_arr=correlate_full_fft(h)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c_arr)): break
       C=c5_from_corr(c_arr,n)
       phi,w=softmax_and_phi(c_arr,n,1000.0)
       if not math.isfinite(phi): break
       grad=grad_from_weights(h,w,n)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       order=np.argsort(grad)
       lows=order[:Q]
       highs=order[-Q:]
       improved=False
       for a in highs:
          ha=float(h[a])
          for b in lows:
             if a==b: continue
             hb=float(h[b])
             maxd=min(ha, 1.0-hb)
             if maxd <=1e-10: continue
             for frac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
                d=maxd*frac
                x=h.copy()
                x[a]=ha-d; x[b]=hb+d
                x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
                cx=correlate_full_fft(x); Cx=c5_from_corr(cx,n)
                if Cx < local_c - 1e-9:
                   Cxex=compute_c5_exact(x)
                   if Cxex < local_c - 1e-12:
                      h=x; local_c=Cxex; local=h.copy(); record_best(h,local_c); improved=True; break
             if improved and Cxex? no
       if improved:
          no_imp=0
       else:
          no_imp+=1
          if no_imp >=2 and remaining()>10:
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
             if i != j:
                 x=h.copy()
                 x[i]=rng.rand(); x[j]=rng.rand()
                 x=project_box_sum(x,target)
                 Cx=compute_c5_exact(x)
                 if Cx < local_c - 1e-12:
                    h=x; local_c=Cx; local=h.copy(); record_best(h,local_c); no_imp=0; continue
          if no_imp >=3: break
    return local, local_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_REC_H = None
_REC_C = 1e100
_REC_N = 0
_START_TIME = 0.0
_BUDGET_S = 1300.0
_SL_N = 0
_SL_BETA = 1.0


def remaining():
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET_S - (time.time() - _START_TIME) - 5.0)


def record_best(h, c=None):
    global _REC_H, _REC_C, _REC_N
    try:
        if h is None or len(h) == 0:
            return
        hq = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), float(len(h)) / 2.0)
        cq = compute_c5_exact(hq)
        if cq < _REC_C:
            _REC_H = hq.copy()
            _REC_C = float(cq)
            _REC_N = int(len(hq))
    except Exception:
        pass


def compute_c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e100
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def correlate_full_fft(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return np.full(2 * n - 1, 1e100, dtype=float)
    v = 1.0 - h
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << max(1, need.bit_length())
    A = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(v, L)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(B), L)
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float)
    r[np.abs(r) < 1e-14] = 0.0
    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        c[:n - 1] = r[L - (n - 1):L]
        c[n - 1] = r[0]
        c[n:] = r[1:n]
    else:
        c[0] = r[0]
    return c


def c5_from_corr(c, n):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def smooth_phi(c, n, beta):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100
    C = c * (2.0 / float(n))
    m = float(np.max(C))
    if beta <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(beta):
        return m
    z = np.exp(beta * (C - m))
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + math.log(s) / beta


def softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(c.size, dtype=float)
    C = c * (2.0 / float(n))
    m = float(np.max(C))
    if beta <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(beta):
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=float)
        return m, w
    z = np.exp(beta * (C - m))
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        z = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=float)
        z[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    phi = m + math.log(s) / beta
    return phi, z / s


def grad_from_weights(h, wfull, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    wfull = np.asarray(wfull, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(wfull)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    if n >= 256:
        need = 2 * n - 1
        L = 1 << max(1, need.bit_length())
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w_rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w0 = wfull[n - 1]
        w[0] = w0
        w_rev[0] = w0
        if n > 1:
            w[1:n] = wfull[n:]
            w[L - (n - 1):L] = wfull[:n - 1]
            w_rev[1:n] = wfull[:n - 1][::-1]
            w_rev[L - (n - 1):L] = wfull[n:][::-1]
        q = 1.0 - h
        A = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w, L) * np.fft.rfft(q, L), L)[:n]
        B = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w_rev, L) * np.fft.rfft(h, L), L)[:n]
        g = dx * (A - B)
        g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
        return g

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w0 = wfull[n - 1]
    if abs(w0) > 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    base = n - 1
    for p in range(1, n):
        wp = wfull[base + p]
        wm = wfull[base - p]
        if abs(wp) <= 1e-16 and abs(wm) <= 1e-16:
            continue
        hb = h[:n - p]
        hs = h[p:]
        omhb = omh[:n - p]
        omhs = omh[p:]
        g[:n - p] += wm * omhs - wp * hs
        g[p:n] += wp * omhb - wm * hb
    g *= dx
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g


def project_box(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return np.zeros(y.shape, dtype=float)
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(y)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    target = float(target)
    if not np.isfinite(target) or target < 0.0:
        target = 0.0
    elif target > float(n):
        target = float(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=float)
    if y.min() >= -1e-9 and y.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-9:
        x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
        hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(64):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = x < 1.0 - 1e-12
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def resample_h(h, dst_n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    src_n = len(h)
    dst_n = int(dst_n)
    if dst_n <= 0:
        dst_n = 8
    if dst_n % 2:
        dst_n += 1
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return project_box_sum(h.copy(), dst_n / 2.0)
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(src_n))
    xn = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(dst_n))
    y = np.interp(xn, xp, h)
    return project_box_sum(y, dst_n / 2.0)


def make_anti_h(z):
    z = project_box(z)
    m = len(z)
    h = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = z
    h[m:] = 1.0 - z
    return h


def make_anti_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    m = n // 2
    if m < 1:
        return make_anti_sine(2, 0.5, 0.0)
    z = 0.5 * (h[:m] + (1.0 - h[m:2 * m]))
    return make_anti_h(z)


def make_anti_sine(n, amp, phase):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 2
    if n % 2:
        n += 1
    m = n // 2
    x = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    z = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(math.pi * x + phase)
    return make_anti_h(z)


def make_full_sine(n, amp, freq, phase):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 2
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + phase)
    return project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)


def make_tent(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 2
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.minimum(x, 2.0 - x)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)


def make_thue_morse(n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        v = i
        cnt = 0
        while v:
            cnt ^= (v & 1)
            v >>= 1
        if cnt:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)


def line_search_full(h, grad, c, C, phi, beta, target):
    n = len(h)
    gnorm2 = float(np.dot(grad, grad))
    if gnorm2 <= 1e-30 or not math.isfinite(gnorm2):
        return h, c, C, phi, False
    alpha = 1.0
    best_x = None
    best_c = None
    best_C = C
    best_phi = phi
    for _ in range(16):
        x = project_box_sum(h - alpha * grad, target)
        cx = correlate_full_fft(x)
        Cx = c5_from_corr(cx, n)
        phix = smooth_phi(cx, n, beta)
        if math.isfinite(phix) and phix < best_phi - 1e-15:
            best_phi = phix
            best_x = x
            best_c = cx
            best_C = Cx
        if (math.isfinite(Cx) and Cx < C - 1e-13) or (
            math.isfinite(phix) and phix < phi - 1e-6 * alpha * gnorm2
        ):
            return x, cx, Cx, phix, True
        alpha *= 0.5
        if alpha < 1e-12:
            break
    if best_x is not None and best_phi < phi - 1e-14:
        return best_x, best_c, best_C, best_phi, True
    return h, c, C, phi, False


def line_search_anti(z, grad_z, c, C, phi, beta):
    m = len(z)
    n = 2 * m
    gnorm2 = float(np.dot(grad_z, grad_z))
    if gnorm2 <= 1e-30 or not math.isfinite(gnorm2):
        return z, c, C, phi, False
    alpha = 1.0
    best_z = None
    best_c = None
    best_C = C
    best_phi = phi
    for _ in range(16):
        xz = project_box(z - alpha * grad_z)
        h = make_anti_h(xz)
        hc = correlate_full_fft(h)
        Cx = c5_from_corr(hc, n)
        phix = smooth_phi(hc, n, beta)
        if math.isfinite(phix) and phix < best_phi - 1e-15:
            best_phi = phix
            best_z = xz
            best_c = hc
            best_C = Cx
        if (math.isfinite(Cx) and Cx < C - 1e-13) or (
            math.isfinite(phix) and phix < phi - 1e-6 * alpha * gnorm2
        ):
            return xz, hc, Cx, phix, True
        alpha *= 0.5
        if alpha < 1e-12:
            break
    if best_z is not None and best_phi < phi - 1e-14:
        return best_z, best_c, best_C, best_phi, True
    return z, c, C, phi, False


def pair_exchange_polish(h, time_limit, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    local = h.copy()
    local_c = compute_c5_exact(local)
    record_best(local, local_c)
    deadline = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    no_imp = 0
    Q = 8 if n <= 200 else 6
    for sweep in range(100000):
        if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 5.0:
            break
        c_arr = correlate_full_fft(local)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c_arr)):
            break
        phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c_arr, n, 1200.0)
        if not math.isfinite(phi):
            break
        grad = grad_from_weights(local, w, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break
        order = np.argsort(grad)
        lows = order[:Q]
        highs = order[-Q:]
        improved = False
        for a in highs:
            ha = float(local[a])
            for b in lows:
                if a == b:
                    continue
                hb = float(local[b])
                maxd = min(ha, 1.0 - hb)
                if maxd <= 1e-10:
                    continue
                for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
                    d = maxd * frac
                    try:
                        x = local.copy()
                        x[a] = ha - d
                        x[b] = hb + d
                        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
                        cx = correlate_full_fft(x)
                        Cx = c5_from_corr(cx, n)
                        if Cx < local_c - 1e-9:
                            Cxex = compute_c5_exact(x)
                            if Cxex < local_c - 1e-12:
                                local = x.copy()
                                local_c = Cxex
                                record_best(local, local_c)
                                improved = True
                                break
                    except Exception:
                        continue
        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 2 and remaining() > 12.0:
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i != j:
                    try:
                        x = local.copy()
                        x[i] = float(rng.rand())
                        x[j] = float(rng.rand())
                        x = project_box_sum(x, target)
                        Cx = compute_c5_exact(x)
                        if Cx < local_c - 1e-12:
                            local = x.copy()
                            local_c = Cx
                            record_best(local, local_c)
                            no_imp = 0
                            continue
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            if no_imp >= 3:
                break
    return local, local_c


def improve_full(h, alloc, beta_sched, rng, do_pair=True):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    c = correlate_full_fft(h)
    C = c5_from_corr(c, n)
    record_best(h, C)
    local = h.copy()
    local_c = compute_c5_exact(local)
    deadline = time.time() + float(alloc)
    eval_every = 5 if n <= 256 else (10 if n <= 512 else 20)
    for beta in beta_sched:
        if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 5.0:
            break
        if beta <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(beta):
            continue
        phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
        if not math.isfinite(phi):
            break
        for it in range(100000):
            if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 3.0:
                break
            grad = grad_from_weights(h, w, n)
            h_new, c_new, C_new, phi_new, ok = line_search_full(
                h, grad, c, C, phi, beta, target
            )
            if not ok:
                break
            h, c, C, phi = h_new, c_new, C_new, phi_new
            if not math.isfinite(C):
                h = local.copy()
                c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                C = local_c
            if C > local_c + 0.005:
                h = local.copy()
                c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                C = local_c
            phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
            if not math.isfinite(phi):
                break
            if (it + 1) % eval_every == 0:
                Cex = compute_c5_exact(h)
                if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
                    local = h.copy()
                    local_c = Cex
                    record_best(local, local_c)
                elif Cex > local_c + 0.003:
                    h = local.copy()
                    c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                    C = local_c
                    phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
        Cex = compute_c5_exact(h)
        if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
            local = h.copy()
            local_c = Cex
            record_best(local, local_c)
        elif Cex > local_c + 0.002:
            h = local.copy()
    if do_pair and n <= 512 and time.time() < deadline - 5.0 and remaining() > 5.0:
        pair_time = min(60.0, max(4.0, deadline - time.time()))
        try:
            h2, c2 = pair_exchange_polish(local.copy(), pair_time, rng)
            if c2 < local_c:
                local = h2.copy()
                local_c = c2
                record_best(local, local_c)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return local, local_c


def improve_anti(z, alloc, beta_sched, rng):
    z = project_box(z)
    m = len(z)
    n = 2 * m
    h = make_anti_h(z)
    c = correlate_full_fft(h)
    C = c5_from_corr(c, n)
    h_feas = project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    local_c = compute_c5_exact(h_feas)
    record_best(h_feas, local_c)
    local_z = z.copy()
    deadline = time.time() + float(alloc)
    eval_every = 5 if n <= 256 else (10 if n <= 512 else 20)
    for beta in beta_sched:
        if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 5.0:
            break
        if beta <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(beta):
            continue
        phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
        if not math.isfinite(phi):
            break
        for it in range(100000):
            if time.time() > deadline or remaining() < 3.0:
                break
            grad_h = grad_from_weights(h, w, n)
            grad_z = grad_h[:m] - grad_h[m:]
            z_new, c_new, C_new, phi_new, ok = line_search_anti(
                z, grad_z, c, C, phi, beta
            )
            if not ok:
                break
            z, c, C, phi = z_new, c_new, C_new, phi_new
            h = make_anti_h(z)
            if not math.isfinite(C):
                z = local_z.copy()
                h = make_anti_h(z)
                c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                C = local_c
            if C > local_c + 0.005:
                z = local_z.copy()
                h = make_anti_h(z)
                c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                C = local_c
            phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
            if not math.isfinite(phi):
                break
            if (it + 1) % eval_every == 0:
                hf = project_box_sum(make_anti_h(z), n / 2.0)
                Cex = compute_c5_exact(hf)
                if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
                    local_z = z.copy()
                    local_c = Cex
                    record_best(hf, local_c)
                elif Cex > local_c + 0.003:
                    z = local_z.copy()
                    h = make_anti_h(z)
                    c = correlate_full_fft(h)
                    C = local_c
                    phi, w = softmax_and_phi(c, n, beta)
        hf = project_box_sum(make_anti_h(z), n / 2.0)
        Cex = compute_c5_exact(hf)
        if Cex < local_c - 1e-15:
            local_z = z.copy()
            local_c = Cex
            record_best(hf, local_c)
        elif Cex > local_c + 0.002:
            z = local_z.copy()
    h_out = project_box_sum(make_anti_h(local_z), n / 2.0)
    c_out = compute_c5_exact(h_out)
    record_best(h_out, c_out)
    return h_out, c_out


def _slsqp_obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or len(x) != _SL_N:
        return 1e100
    c = correlate_full_fft(x)
    return smooth_phi(c, _SL_N, _SL_BETA)


def _slsqp_jac(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or len(x) != _SL_N:
        return np.zeros(_SL_N, dtype=float)
    c = correlate_full_fft(x)
    _, w = softmax_and_phi(c, _SL_N, _SL_BETA)
    return grad_from_weights(x, w, _SL_N)


def _slsqp_con(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return float(np.sum(x) - _SL_N / 2.0)


def slsqp_polish(h, beta, maxiter):
    global _SL_N, _SL_BETA
    n = len(h)
    x0 = project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    _SL_N = n
    _SL_BETA = float(beta)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _slsqp_con}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _slsqp_obj,
            x0,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-14, "iprint": 0},
        )
        x = project_box_sum(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n / 2.0)
        record_best(x, None)
        return x
    except Exception:
        return x0


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _START_TIME, _BUDGET_S, _REC_H, _REC_C, _REC_N
    _START_TIME = time.time()
    _BUDGET_S = float(budget_s)
    _REC_H = None
    _REC_C = 1e100
    _REC_N = 0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        ph = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if ph.size > 0:
            parent = project_box_sum(ph.copy(), len(ph) / 2.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = make_anti_sine(160, 0.5, 0.0)

    cpar = compute_c5_exact(parent)
    record_best(parent, cpar)

    beta_full = [35.0, 90.0, 220.0, 550.0, 1400.0, 3200.0]
    beta_mid = [50.0, 150.0, 450.0, 1200.0]
    beta_short = [80.0, 250.0, 700.0]

    def try_full(h, alloc, schedule, do_pair=True):
        if h is None or remaining() <= alloc + 5.0:
            return
        alloc = min(alloc, remaining() - 6.0)
        if alloc < 8.0:
            return
        try:
            h2, c2 = improve_full(h.copy(), alloc, schedule, rng, do_pair)
            if c2 < _REC_C:
                record_best(h2, c2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def try_anti(z, alloc, schedule):
        if z is None or remaining() <= alloc + 5.0:
            return
        alloc = min(alloc, remaining() - 6.0)
        if alloc < 8.0:
            return
        try:
            h2, c2 = improve_anti(z.copy(), alloc, schedule, rng)
            if c2 < _REC_C:
                record_best(h2, c2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Parent and direct anti-symmetrized parent.
    try_full(parent.copy(), 300.0, beta_full, True)
    if parent.size >= 2 and remaining() > 80.0:
        try_anti(make_anti_from_h(parent)[: parent.size // 2], 120.0, beta_mid)

    # Higher resolutions by interpolation.
    for _ in range(2):
        if remaining() <= 200.0 or _REC_H is None:
            break
        nn = int(_REC_N)
        if nn < 2:
            break
        target_n = 2 * nn
        if target_n > 1024 or target_n == nn:
            break
        h_hi = resample_h(_REC_H.copy(), target_n)
        try_full(h_hi, 260.0, beta_full, target_n <= 512)
        if int(_REC_N) >= target_n:
            continue
        if remaining() <= 160.0:
            break

    # Coarser resolution, which can sometimes reduce the discrete maximum.
    if _REC_H is not None and _REC_N >= 96 and remaining() > 70.0:
        try_full(resample_h(_REC_H.copy(), max(64, _REC_N // 2)), 80.0, beta_short, True)

    # Structured samplers from different basins.
    if remaining() > 40.0:
        seeds = []
        for n0 in (160, 320):
            for amp, phase in (
                (0.50, 0.0),
                (0.50, math.pi / 4.0),
                (0.47, math.pi / 3.0),
                (0.42, 0.7),
                (0.38, math.pi / 2.0),
            ):
                seeds.append(make_anti_sine(n0, amp, phase))
            seeds.append(make_full_sine(n0, 0.45, 1.0, 0.6))
            seeds.append(make_full_sine(n0, 0.42, 2.0, 1.1))
        for sp in seeds:
            if remaining() <= 24.0:
                break
            try_full(sp, 22.0, beta_short, len(sp) <= 512)

    if remaining() > 35.0:
        try_full(make_tent(160), 30.0, beta_short, True)
    if remaining() > 25.0:
        try_full(make_thue_morse(160), 22.0, beta_short, True)
    if remaining() > 25.0:
        try_full(make_thue_morse(256), 22.0, beta_short, True)

    # Final SLSQP polish at a modest resolution.
    if _REC_H is not None:
        nn = int(_REC_N)
        if remaining() > 90.0 and nn <= 128:
            try:
                x = slsqp_polish(_REC_H.copy(), 1800.0, 60)
                record_best(x, None)
            except Exception:
                pass
        if remaining() > 60.0 and 129 <= nn <= 224:
            try:
                x = slsqp_polish(_REC_H.copy(), 1200.0, 30)
                record_best(x, None)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if _REC_H is None:
        hout = make_anti_sine(160, 0.5, 0.0)
        hout = project_box_sum(hout, 80.0)
        cout = compute_c5_exact(hout)
        return hout, float(cout), 160

    hout = project_box_sum(np.asarray(_REC_H, dtype=float).copy(), _REC_N / 2.0)
    cout = compute_c5_exact(hout)
    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(hout)
        if ec is not None and math.isfinite(float(ec)) and float(ec) < cout:
            cout = float(ec)
    except Exception:
        pass
    if cout >= _REC_C - 1e-12:
        cout = float(_REC_C)
    return hout, float(cout), int(_REC_N)
```
